    (function ($) {
        volusion.ready(function () {
            if (volusion.cart.isObservingCount()) {
                var ts = new Date().getTime();
                $.getJSON('/ajaxcart.asp?cachebust=' + ts, function (data) {
                    var quantityTotal = 0;
                    $.each(data.Products, function (key, val) {
                        if (val.IsProduct === 'Y') {
                            quantityTotal += parseInt(val.Quantity);
                        } else if (val.IsAccessory === 'Y') {
                            quantityTotal += parseInt(val.Quantity);
                        }
                    });
                    quantityTotal = quantityTotal || '0';
                    volusion.cart.itemCount(quantityTotal);
                });
            }
        });
    } (jQuery));
</script>

<script>
function getQueryVariable(variable)
{
	try {
		 var query = window.location.search.substring(1);
		 var vars = query.split("&");
		 for (var i=0;i<vars.length;i++) {
				 var pair = vars[i].split("=");
				 if(pair[0] == variable){return pair[1];}
		 }
		 return(false);
     }
     catch(e) {
//		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
//		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
//		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
//		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
     }
}

function sizeImages() {

	try {      

		var images = document.getElementsByTagName('img'); 
		for(var i = 0; i < images.length; i++) {
			var img = images[i];
	
			if ( img.src.indexOf("btn_go_gray.gif") != -1 ) {
				img.style.visibility = 'hidden';
			}

// this makes a bad image show the "no photo" image instead - much better this way
img.onerror = function () {
   this.src = '/v/vspfiles/templates/260/images/nophoto.gif';
};

			if ( img.className == "vCSS_img_product_photo_small" && ( img.src.indexOf('._SL75_') > -1 ||  img.src.indexOf('._SL160_')  > -1  ) ) {

				var old_src = img.src;
				img.src = img.src.replace('._SL75_', '');
				img.src = img.src.replace('._SL160_', '');
				var x = img.src;
				if ( x.indexOf("no-img-sm" ) > 0 || x.indexOf("nophoto.gif" ) > 0 ) {
					img.src = "https://www.discoverbooks.com/v/no-image.jpg";
				}
				else
				{
					//$("img[src$='"++"']")
					$('a[href*="'+old_src +'"]').attr("href",img.src);
					$('a[href*="'+old_src +'"]').parent().parent().parent().parent().parent().width("40%");
				}

			} else if  ( img.src.indexOf('._SL75_')> -1  ||  img.src.indexOf('._SL160_')> -1  )     {
				img.src = img.src.replace('._SL75_','._SL160_');

				var x = img.src;

				if ( x.indexOf("no-img-sm" ) > 0 || x.indexOf("nophoto.gif" ) > 0 ) {
					img.src = "https://www.discoverbooks.com/v/no-image.jpg";
				}
			}
		}


	}
	catch(e) {
		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
	}            

setTimeout( sizeImages, 4000 );
}

{
    try {
//var width = window.innerWidth;
var width = window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth || document.body.clientWidth;

if(typeof global_Current_ProductCode=== "undefined"){
  /* It is undefined */
}
else
{

/* On the product details page, this expands/collapses the Book Details area */
$(".book_desc_header").click(function () {
    $header = $(this);
    //getting the next element
    $content = $header.next();
    //open up the content needed - toggle the slide- if visible, slide up, if not slidedown.
    $content.slideToggle(500, function () {
        //execute this after slideToggle is done
        //change text of header based on visibility of content div
        $header.text(function () {
            //change text based on condition
            return $content.is(":visible") ? " - Hide Book Details " : " + Show Book Details";
        });
    });
});

}

function checkGoogleShoppingRefer()
{
	var gclid = getQueryVariable("gclid");
	if (gclid != null && gclid.length > 0  )
	{
	  setTimeout(function() { googleAlert(); }, 1000);
	}
}

}

function showCartCoupons()
{

                        var countProducts = 0;

                        {
                              if ( isNaN($("#Quantity"+(idx+1)).val() ) )
                              {
                                   break;
                              }
                              //alert( parseInt($("#Quantity"+(idx+1)).val() ));
                              countProducts = countProducts  + parseInt($("#Quantity"+(idx+1)).val());
                        }

                        if ( countProducts  < 3 && countProducts  > 0  )
                        {
$(".v65-your-cart-title").after("    <p>    	<h3><font color='red'>Did you know?</font>&nbsp;&nbsp;If you buy 3 or more books, you could save <font color='red'>5%</font> with this coupon code <font color='red'>THREE5</font>.&nbsp;&nbsp;<font size='3' color='#6E2B62'>Discount applies to item prices; cannot be combined with other offers or coupons.</font></h3>	</p>");

                        }

var toggleCheckboxOnce = 0;

function  toggleCheckbox(checkbx)
{
	try 
	{
		var bbvalue = checkbx.value;
		var bbpc = bbvalue.substring(0,10);

		if ( toggleCheckboxOnce == 0 )
		{
			//alert(bbvalue) ;
			//alert(bbpc ) ;

			toggleCheckboxOnce =1;
			if ( bbvalue != bbpc+"-New")  
			{
			//	alert("new");
				var bbfind = bbpc+"-New";
				//alert(bbfind );
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").attr("checked",false);
				//$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").parent().parent().parent().css("background-color","white");
			}
			if ( bbvalue.indexOf("-Like-New") == -1  )
			{
			//	alert("like new");
				var bbfind = bbpc+"-Like-New";
				//alert(bbfind );
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").attr("checked",false);
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").parent().parent().parent().css("background-color","white");
			}
			if ( bbvalue.indexOf("-Very-Good") == -1  )
			{
				//alert("very good");
				var bbfind = bbpc+"-Very-Good";
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").attr("checked",false);
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").parent().parent().parent().css("background-color","white");
			}
			if ( bbvalue.indexOf("-Good") == -1  )
			{
				//alert("good");
				var bbfind = bbpc+"-Good";
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").attr("checked",false);
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").parent().parent().parent().css("background-color","white");
			//	$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").parent().parent().animate( { background-color: 'white' } , 500 );
			}
			if ( bbvalue.indexOf("-Acceptable") == -1  )
			{
				//alert("Acceptable");
				var bbfind = bbpc+"-Acceptable";
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").attr("checked",false);
				$(":checkbox[value="+bbfind +"]").parent().parent().parent().css("background-color","white");
			}
		}

			if ( bbvalue != bbpc+"-New")  
		{
			if ( checkbx.checked == true )
			{
				checkbx.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.style="border: solid thin LightGray;background-color:#B1E362;";
			}
			else
			{
				checkbx.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.style="border: solid thin LightGray;background-color:white;";
			}
		}
		return true;
	}
	catch(e) 
	{
		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
	}
}

function changeIsbn13to10()
{

// ******  BEGIN SEARCH BAR HIJACK FOR ISBN13
try {
function processForm(e) {

    if (e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();

//var searchString = document.getElementById("search").value;
var searchString = document.getElementsByClassName("search__input")[0].value;
//alert("searchString=" + searchString);
var re = new RegExp(' ', 'g');
var re2 = new RegExp('-', 'g');

searchString = searchString.replace(re, "" );
searchString = searchString.replace(re2, "" );

if ( searchString.length == 13 &&  searchString.substring(0, 3) == "978" )
{

var isbn10 = searchString.substring(3, 13);
 
	   var  checkdigit = 11 - ( ( 10 * (1*isbn10.substring(0,1)) + 9 * (1*isbn10.substring(1,2)) + 8 * (1*isbn10.substring(2,3)) + 7 * (1*isbn10.substring(3,4)) + 6 * (1*isbn10.substring(4,5)) + 5 * (1*isbn10.substring(5,6)) + 4 * (1*isbn10.substring(6,7)) + 3 * (1*isbn10.substring(7,8)) + 2 * (1*isbn10.substring(8,9)) ) % 11);

	    //
	    // convert the numeric check value
	    // into the single char version
	    //
	    if ( checkdigit ==10  ) 
	    {
				checkdigit = "X";
                   }
              else if (checkdigit ==11) 
               {			
				checkdigit = 0;
	    }
//alert("check = " + checkdigit );
isbn10 = searchString.substring(3, 12) +  checkdigit ;

document.getElementsByClassName("search__input")[0].value = isbn10 ;
 $(".search__form").submit();

} else 
{
 var searchString2 = document.getElementsByClassName("search__input")[0].value;
searchString2 = searchString2.replace(" by ", " " );
searchString2 = searchString2.replace(" BY ", " " );
document.getElementsByClassName("search__input")[0].value = searchString2 ;
 $(".search__form").submit();
}

}

//var form = document.getElementById('SearchBoxFormMain');
var form = document.getElementsByClassName("search__form")[0];
if (form.attachEvent) {
//alert(1);
    form.attachEvent("submit", processForm);
} else {
//alert(2);
    form.addEventListener("submit", processForm);
}
}
catch(e) {
var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error=2"+my_err );
}
// ******  END SEARCH BAR HIJACK FOR ISBN13


}


function doNotLoadChildProducts()
{
	// ***************************BEGIN  do not load a child page
	try {
	//alert(location.pathname + "      ----       " +  location.href   + "      ----       " +   location.href.indexOf("-Good"));
		if 
		(  
			(      ( location.pathname.indexOf("p/") != -1 && location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("p/")  ) || 		 
                        	location.pathname.indexOf("ProductDetails.asp") != -1 || 
				location.pathname.indexOf("productdetails.asp") != -1 ||
				location.pathname.indexOf("product_p") != -1 ||
				location.pathname.indexOf("product-p") != -1 
			 )
		&&
			(
			location.href.indexOf("-new.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-like-new.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-very-good.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-good.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-acceptable.htm") != -1 ||
			location.href.indexOf("-New.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-Like-New.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-Very-Good.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-Good.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("-Acceptable.htm") != -1 ||
			location.href.indexOf("%2Dnew.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2Dlike%2Dnew.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2Dvery%2Dgood.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2Dgood.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2Dacceptable.htm") != -1 ||
			location.href.indexOf("%2DNew.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2DLike%2DNew.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2DVery%2DGood.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2DGood.htm") != -1  || 
			location.href.indexOf("%2DAcceptable.htm") != -1 
			)
		)
		{
			var url_product =location.href;
			//alert("in first if url="+url_product);
			if 
			( 
				url_product.indexOf("%2Dacceptable.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-acceptable.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2Dgood.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-good.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2Dvery%2Dgood.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-very-good.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2Dlike%2Dnew.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-like-new.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2Dnew.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-new.htm") != -1  ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2DAcceptable.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-Acceptable.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2DGood.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-Good.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2DVery%2DGood.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-Very-Good.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2DLike%2DNew.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-Like-New.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("%2DNew.htm") != -1 ||
				url_product.indexOf("-New.htm") != -1
			)
			{
				//alert("in second if");
				var my_condition = "";
				if 
				( 
					url_product.indexOf("%2Dacceptable.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-acceptable.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("%2DAcceptable.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-Acceptable.htm") != -1
				)
				{
					my_condition = "Acceptable";
				}
				if 
				( 
					url_product.indexOf("%2Dgood.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-good.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("%2DGood.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-Good.htm") != -1 
				)
				{
					my_condition = "Good";
				}
				if 
				( 
					url_product.indexOf("%2Dvery%2Dgood.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-very-good.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("%2DVery%2DGood.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-Very-Good.htm") != -1  
				)
				{
					my_condition = "VeryGood";
				}
				if 
				( 
					url_product.indexOf("-new.htm") != -1  ||
					url_product.indexOf("%2Dnew.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("%2DNew.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-New.htm") != -1   
				)
				{
					my_condition = "New";
				}
				if 
				( 
					url_product.indexOf("%2Dlike%2Dnew.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-like-new.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("-Like-New.htm") != -1 ||
					url_product.indexOf("%2DNew.htm") != -1 
				)
				{
					my_condition = "LikeNew";
				}


				url_product = url_product.replace("%2DLike%2DNew", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-Like-New", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2DNew", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-New", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2DVery%2DGood", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-Very-Good", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2DGood", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-Good", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2DAcceptable", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-Acceptable", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2Dlike%2Dnew", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-like-new", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2Dnew", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-new", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2Dvery%2Dgood", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-very-good", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2Dgood", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-good", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("%2Dacceptable", "");
				url_product = url_product.replace("-acceptable", "");

				var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
				xhr.open("POST", "/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/set_session_var.asp?name=cond&value="+my_condition, true);
				xhr.send();
				document.location =url_product
			}
		}
	}
	catch(e) {
		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
	}
	// ***************************END  do not load a child page
}

function checkSelectedProductConditionAndQuantity()
{
	var desired_qty = $(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val();
        var acceptable_count = $('.acceptable_count').html();
        var good_count= $('.good_count').html();
        var verygood_count= $('.verygood_count').html();
        var likenew_count= $('.likenew_count').html();

        // need to see if it's checked AND the QTY value exceeds what we have in stock - prevent this from happening
	if($("input[value='186']").is(':checked') && desired_qty*1 >  acceptable_count*1  && acceptable_count != null ) 
	{ 
		var msg = "Only "+acceptable_count+" acceptable copies of this book are available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		if (acceptable_count*1 == 1)
		{
			msg = "Only "+acceptable_count+" acceptable copy of this book is available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		}
		alert( msg);
		$(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val(acceptable_count);
        }
	if($("input[value='185']").is(':checked') && desired_qty*1 >  good_count*1   && good_count != null  ) 
	{ 
		var msg = "Only "+good_count+" good copies of this book are available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		if (good_count*1 == 1)
		{
			msg = "Only "+good_count+" good copy of this book is available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		}
		alert( msg);
		$(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val(good_count);
        }
	if($("input[value='184']").is(':checked') && desired_qty*1 >  verygood_count*1    && verygood_count != null ) 
	{ 
		var msg = "Only "+verygood_count+" very good copies of this book are available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		if (verygood_count*1 == 1)
		{
			msg = "Only "+verygood_count+" very good copy of this book is available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		}
		alert( msg);
		$(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val(verygood_count);
        }
	if($("input[value='183']").is(':checked') && desired_qty*1 >  likenew_count*1   && likenew_count != null  ) 
	{ 
		var msg = "Only "+likenew_count+" like new copies of this book are available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		if (likenew_count*1 == 1)
		{
			msg = "Only "+likenew_count+" like new copy of this book is available.  We are resetting the selected quantity.";
		}
		alert( msg);
		$(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val(likenew_count);
        }


	defaultCheckMark();

	setTimeout(checkSelectedProductConditionAndQuantity, 200);
	
	
}


function defaultCheckMark()
{
	if (     ( location.pathname.indexOf("p/") != -1 && location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("p/")  ) || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ProductDetails.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("productdetails.asp") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product_p") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product-p") != -1 
	)
	{
	        var acceptable_count = $('.acceptable_count').html();
	        var good_count= $('.good_count').html();
	        var verygood_count= $('.verygood_count').html();
	        var likenew_count= $('.likenew_count').html();
		if(!$("input[value='183']").is(':checked') && !$("input[value='184']").is(':checked') && !$("input[value='185']").is(':checked') && !$("input[value='186']").is(':checked') )
		{
			if ( likenew_count*1 > 0 ) {
				$("input[value='183']").attr('checked', true);
			} else if ( verygood_count*1 > 0 ) {
				$("input[value='184']").attr('checked', true);
			} else if ( good_count*1 > 0 ) {
				$("input[value='185']").attr('checked', true);
			} else if ( acceptable_count *1 > 0 ) {
				$("input[value='186']").attr('checked', true);
			} 
		}
	}
}

	// do nothing
	}


}

});

});

}


function moveBuyBoxAboveDescription() 
{
	if (      ( location.pathname.indexOf("p/") != -1 && location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("p/")  ) || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ProductDetails.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("productdetails.asp") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product_p") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product-p") != -1 
	)
	{
		if ( $('.colors_pricebox').length == 2 ) 
		{
				var desc_box = $(".colors_pricebox").eq(0).html();
				$(".colors_pricebox").eq(0).html("");
				$(".colors_pricebox").eq(1).after('<table width="100%" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" class="colors_pricebox">'+desc_box 	+ '</table>');
		}
	}
}



function getCartCount()
{

$("body").after("<div  style='visibility: hidden' id='cartcount'></div>");
$("#cartcount").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/get_session_cart.asp?name=cart");
var count = $("#cartcount").html();
//alert ( "session count = " + count );
if ( count == "" ) {
     count = $('span[data-v-observable]').html();
//alert ( "saving session count = " + count );
	$("#cartcount").load( "/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/set_session_cart.asp?name=cart&value="+count );
}
else
{
	currentcount = $('span[data-v-observable]').html();
//alert ( "currentcount  = " + currentcount );
	if ( currentcount  != count )
	{
//		alert( " open the side cart and save" );
	$("#cartcount").load( "/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/set_session_cart.asp?name=cart&value="+count );
		
	}
}
//alert($('span[data-v-observable]').html());
}

function collapseBookDetails()
{
	$('span[itemprop="description"]').wrap('<div class="book_desc_container"><div class="book_desc_header" style="text-align: center;"><span class="show_hide_book_details"> + Show Book Details</span></div><div class="book_desc_content" style="display:none"><table width="100%" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" class="colors_pricebox">' + $('span[itemprop="description"]').html() );
	$('span[itemprop="description"]').hide();
$(".book_desc_container").hide();
$("#alsoBought").before($('.book_desc_container').html() + "<p> <br>");
//$("#alsoBought").before( " BRYAN TEST" );
}

}



function productPageAddToCartAndCheckoutButton()
{
	if (       ( location.pathname.indexOf("p/") != -1 && location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("p/")  ) || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ProductDetails.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("productdetails.asp") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product_p") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product-p") != -1 
	)
	{
		//$("#v65-product-wishlist-button").before( '<a id="btn_addAndCheckout" name="btn_addAndCheckout"  href="/ShoppingCart.asp?ProductCode=0307474275-0003&checkout=1" class="vCSS_input_addtocart btn btn-primary btn-lg btn_addAndCheckout">Add & Check Out</a><br> '  );
		//$("#v65-product-wishlist-button").before( '<a id="btn_addAndCheckout" name="btn_addAndCheckout"  href="javascript:SoftAddSingleItem(\'0307474275-0003\', 1, 0); document.location=\'/one-page-checkout.asp\'" class="vCSS_input_addtocart btn btn-primary btn-lg btn_addAndCheckout">Add & Check Out</a><br> '  );

		var productCode = global_Current_ProductCode ;
//alert(1);
		var qty =  $(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val(); ;
//alert(2);
		if ( $("input[value='183']").is(':checked') )
		{
			productCode = productCode + "-0004";
		} else if ( $("input[value='183']").is(':checked') )
		{
			productCode = productCode + "-0003";
		} else if ( $("input[value='183']").is(':checked') )
		{
			productCode = productCode + "-0002";
		} else if ( $("input[value='183']").is(':checked') )
		{
			productCode = productCode + "-0001";
		}  
//alert( "productCode ="+productCode  ) ;
		$("#v65-product-wishlist-button").before( '<a style="background-image: url(https://www.discoverbooks.com/v/arrow_white.png); background-repeat: no-repeat;  background-position: left;" id="btn_addAndCheckout" name="btn_addAndCheckout"  href="javascript:cartAndCheckout(\'"+productCode +"\', "+qty+"); " class="vCSS_input_addtocart btn btn-primary btn-lg btn_addAndCheckout">Buy Now</a><br> '  );
	
	}
}

function updateAddAndCheckoutJS()
{
	if (      ( location.pathname.indexOf("p/") != -1 && location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("p/")  ) || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ProductDetails.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("productdetails.asp") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product_p") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product-p") != -1 
	)
	{
		var productCode = global_Current_ProductCode ;
		var qty= $(".v65-productdetail-cartqty.form-control").val(); ;
		if ( $("input[value='183']").is(':checked') )
		{
//alert(4);
			productCode = productCode + "-0004";
		} else if ( $("input[value='184']").is(':checked') )
		{
//alert(3);
			productCode = productCode + "-0003";
		} else if ( $("input[value='185']").is(':checked') )
		{
//alert(2);
			productCode = productCode + "-0002";
		} else if ( $("input[value='186']").is(':checked') )
		{
//alert(1);

			productCode = productCode + "-0001";
		} 
//alert("code="+productCode +" qty= "+qty );
		$("#btn_addAndCheckout").attr("href", "javascript:cartAndCheckout(\'"+productCode +"\', "+qty + " );" );
		
		setTimeout(updateAddAndCheckoutJS, 100 );
	}

}


function checkOutExpress()
{
	if (
		location.pathname.indexOf("shoppingcart.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ShoppingCart.asp") != -1 
	)
	{
		if ( localStorage['checkout'] == "yes" )
		{
		   localStorage['checkout'] = "no";
		   document.location = "/one-page-checkout.asp";
		}
	}
}


function removeLocalStorage() {

	if (   
		location.pathname.indexOf("shoppingcart.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ShoppingCart.asp") != -1 
	)
	{ 
		//do nothing
	}
	else
	{ 
		localStorage['checkout'] = "no" ;
	}
}

function checkCartExpress()
{
	if (   localStorage['checkout'] == "yes" && (
		location.pathname.indexOf("shoppingcart.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ShoppingCart.asp") != -1  )
	)
	{ 
		$(".myoverlay").show();
	}
}

function wishListText()
{
try {
	if (     ( location.pathname.indexOf("p/") != -1 && location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("p/")  ) || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("ProductDetails.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("productdetails.asp") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product_p") != -1 ||
		location.pathname.indexOf("product-p") != -1 
	)
	{
		if( $("#v65-product-wishlist-button").val() != "Save to Must Read List" ) 
		{ 
			setTimeout(wishListText, 200 );
			$("#v65-product-wishlist-button").val("Save to Must Read List");
		}
	}
	}
	catch(e) {
		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
	}          
}

function wishlistRemove()
{
try {
	if (
		location.pathname.indexOf("WishList.asp") != -1 || 
		location.pathname.indexOf("wishlist.asp") != -1 
	)
	{
		var remove = $('.btn_addtowishlist_remove').html();
		var clear = $('.btn_addtowishlist_clear').html();
		if( !remove || !clear || remove.indexOf( "Remove from List") == -1  || clear.indexOf( "Clear All") == -1  ) 
		{ 
			setTimeout(wishlistRemove, 200 );
			$('.btn_addtowishlist_remove').html( $('.btn_addtowishlist_remove').html().replace(' Remove from wishlist ', 'Remove from List') );
			$('.btn_addtowishlist_clear').html( $('.btn_addtowishlist_clear').html().replace( ' Clear entire wishlist ', ' Clear All ') );
		}
	}

	}
	catch(e) {
		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
	}          
}

function addBindingFilter()
{
var href = window.location.href;
//&f-Paperback=2488
var binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Paperback");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Paperback="+binding_filter , "");
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Hardcover");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Hardcover="+binding_filter , "");
}
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Board%20Book");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Board%20Book="+binding_filter , "");
}
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Mass%20Market%20Paperback");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Mass%20Market%20Paperback="+binding_filter , "");
}
}

if ( binding_filter == false && href.indexOf( "&f-School%20" ) > 0 ) {
     binding_filter = "found";
     href = href.substring( 0, href.indexOf( "f-School%20" ) );
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Spiral%20Bound");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Spiral%20Bound="+binding_filter , "");
}
}


if ( binding_filter == false )
{
//$(".search_results_section table table table tbody tr").after( 
//$(".search_results_section table table table tbody tr").after(  "     <tr>    <td width='100%' valign='middle'><table><tr><td colspan=100%><strong>Choose Binding:</strong></td></tr><tr><td style='font-size: 12px; padding: 4px;'><input type='radio' onchange='if (this.value != \"\") {Add_Search_Param(\"f-\" + this.value.split(\"|\")[1], this.value.split(\"|\")[0]); Refine();}' value='102914|Paperback'> Paperback</td><td style='font-size: 12px; padding: 4px;'><input type='radio' onchange='if (this.value != \"\") {Add_Search_Param(\"f-\" + this.value.split(\"|\")[1], this.value.split(\"|\")[0]); Refine();}' value='102916|Mass Market Paperback'> Mass Market Paperback</td></tr><tr><td style='font-size: 12px; padding: 4px;'><input type='radio' onchange='if (this.value != \"\") {Add_Search_Param(\"f-\" + this.value.split(\"|\")[1], this.value.split(\"|\")[0]); Refine();}' value='102917|Board Book'> Board Book</td><td style='font-size: 12px; padding: 4px;'><input type='radio' onchange='if (this.value != \"\") {Add_Search_Param(\"f-\" + this.value.split(\"|\")[1], this.value.split(\"|\")[0]); Refine();}' value='102915|Hardcover'> Hardcover</td></tr><tr><td style='font-size: 12px; padding: 4px;'><input type='radio' onchange='if (this.value != \"\") {Add_Search_Param(\"f-\" + this.value.split(\"|\")[1], this.value.split(\"|\")[0]); Refine();}' value='102918|School &amp; Library'> School & Library</td><td style='font-size: 12px; padding: 4px;'><input type='radio' onchange='if (this.value != \"\") {Add_Search_Param(\"f-\" + this.value.split(\"|\")[1], this.value.split(\"|\")[0]); Refine();}' value='102919|Spiral Bound'> Spiral Bound</td></tr></table></td></tr>         "  );

function checkForNoProductsDueToBindingFilter()
{
var href = window.location.href;
//&f-Paperback=2488
var binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Paperback");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Paperback="+binding_filter , "");
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Hardcover");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Hardcover="+binding_filter , "");
}
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Board%20Book");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Board%20Book="+binding_filter , "");
}
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Mass%20Market%20Paperback");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Mass%20Market%20Paperback="+binding_filter , "");
}
}

if ( binding_filter == false && href.indexOf( "&f-School%20" ) > 0 ) {
     binding_filter = "found";
     href = href.substring( 0, href.indexOf( "f-School%20" ) );
}

if ( binding_filter == false ) {
     binding_filter = getQueryVariable("f-Spiral%20Bound");
if (  binding_filter != false) {
     href = href.replace( "f-Spiral%20Bound="+binding_filter , "");
}
}
if ( binding_filter != false && !$('.v65-productDisplay')[0]) {
$('#content_area').after("<h4><a href='"+href +"' >← No books found with this binding.  Click here to go back to previous results.</a></h4>");
}



}



function categoryPopluarAuthors()
{

/*  for category pages - this loads most popular author links*/
try
{
  if (   location.pathname.indexOf("s/") != -1 &&
          location.pathname.indexOf(".htm") > location.pathname.indexOf("s/") )
     {
          var ind1 = location.pathname.indexOf("s/");
          var ind2 =location.pathname.indexOf(".htm"); 
          var catId = location.pathname.substr( ind1 +2 ,  (ind2 - ind1 -2) );
          if ( !isNaN(parseFloat(catId )) && isFinite(catId ) &&  ( ( (1.0*catId)  < 102864) || ((1.0*catId) > 102877 )) 
                &&    ( ( (1.0*catId)  < 102879 ) || ((1.0*catId) > 102901 ))
             )
          {
                    //$('.v-product-grid').before('<div id=popularCategoryAuthors/>');
                    //$('#popularCategoryAuthors').load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/popularCategoryAuthors.asp?catid="+catId );
                    $('#popularCategoryAuthors').load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/popularCategoryAuthorsSide.asp?catid="+catId );
          }
     }
}
catch(e) {
var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
}


}


//  BEGIN- make FLOATING DIV for the Binding selection
var mywidth = 137;
function floatingDiv()
{
try {

mywidth = $(".sideBindingDiv").outerWidth();
//alert(mywidth );
if (  1 ) {

 var $scrollingDiv = $(".sideBindingDiv");
//alert($scrollingDiv.html());
var $bottom_visible = 0;
var $top_visible = 1;
$(window).scroll(function(){   
if ( $scrollingDiv == null || $scrollingDiv.offset() == null )
{
return;
}  
var $left_px = $scrollingDiv.offset().left;

var products_top = $(".myProductsResults").offset().top;
var products_bottom = $(".myProductsResults").offset().top + $(".myProductsResults").outerHeight(true);
//var scroll_bottom = $scrollingDiv.position().top + $scrollingDiv.outerHeight(true);
//alert(products_bottom - $(window).scrollTop() );

var distance_from_products_bottom_to_Screen_bottom = products_bottom  - $(window).height()  - $(window).scrollTop();

    var docViewTop = $(window).scrollTop();
    var docViewBottom = docViewTop + $(window).height();

    var elemTop =  $scrollingDiv.offset().top;
    var elemBottom = elemTop + $scrollingDiv.height();
scroll_bottom  = elemBottom ;
//    var products_bottom = products_top + $scrollingDiv.height();
var dif_bottom = docViewBottom - elemBottom ;
var dif_top = docViewTop - elemTop ;

var bottom_visible = 1;


if ( elemBottom - $(window).scrollTop()  > $(window).height() )
{
//alert( "elemBottom = "+ elemBottom  + " window height = " + $(window).height() ) ;
bottom_visible = 0;
}


//alert( "$(window).scrollTop()="+ $(window).scrollTop()+ " products_top = " + products_top) ;
if ( ( products_top - $(window).scrollTop()  ) < 10  )
{
$top_visible= 0;
}
else
{
$top_visible= 1;
}
//alert($(".myProductsResults").offset().left );
if ( !$scrollingDiv.is(':visible') )
{
$top_visible= 1;
}




if ( (  $top_visible == 0 ) )       {
$top_visible = 0;
if ( mywidth < 130 )
{
mywidth = 134;
}

$scrollingDiv.css("position",'fixed' ) ;
$scrollingDiv.css("float","right" );    
$scrollingDiv.css("width", mywidth + "px" );    
//$scrollingDiv.css("padding-left","7px" );    
//$scrollingDiv.css("padding-right:","2px" );    
$scrollingDiv.css("border","1px gray solid" );    
$(".myProductsResults").css("float","right");
$(".myProductsResults").css("width","83%");
if ( scroll_bottom + 60> products_bottom ) // && bottom_visible == 1 )
{
//alert("1 products_bottom  = " + products_bottom + " scroll_bottom="+scroll_bottom + " bottom_visible=" + bottom_visible );
var dist = ( distance_from_products_bottom_to_Screen_bottom + $(window).height() - $scrollingDiv.height()  ) + "px";
if  ( distance_from_products_bottom_to_Screen_bottom + $(window).height() - $scrollingDiv.height()  < 138 )
{
    //dist = "148px";
}
$scrollingDiv.css("top",  dist ) ;
}
else
{
$scrollingDiv.css("top",  "10px" ) ;
//alert("2 products_bottom  = " + products_bottom + " scroll_bottom="+scroll_bottom + " bottom_visible=" + bottom_visible );
}

        } else {
$top_visible = 1;
$scrollingDiv.css("top",products_top - $(window).scrollTop()+"px" )             ;
//$(".myProductsResults").css("float","right");
$(".myProductsResults").css("width","");

        }




        });
}
}
catch(e) {

}
//  END- make FLOATING DIV for the Binding selection
}






function checkoutPageStyleChanges() {
        $("#v65-onepage-CopyBillingToShippingLink").css("cssText", "color: white !important; padding: 2px 3px; font-weight: normal;");
        $("#v65-onepage-CopyBillingToShippingLink").addClass("btn");
        $("#v65-onepage-CopyBillingToShippingLink").addClass("v-form__button-img");
        $("#v65-onepage-CopyBillingToShippingLink").addClass("btn-primary");
        setTimeout(  checkoutPageStyleChanges, 2000 );
}

// ******* BEGIN CHECKOUT SHIPPING DEFAULT
function upsellShipping()
{

try 
{
//default the shipping method to Free Shipping -- if no choice has been made yet
if (  "PLEASE SELECT" == $('[name="ShippingSpeedChoice"] option:selected').text() )
{
    $('[name="ShippingSpeedChoice"]').find("option:contains('Free Shipping')").attr('selected', true);
}


} catch (e)
{
var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
}
setTimeout(upsellShipping, 1000);
}
// ******* END CHECKOUT SHIPPING DEFAULT

function resizeLogoAndSearchBox()
{
$(".logo-wrapper").removeClass("col-md-8");
$(".logo-wrapper").addClass("col-md-6");
$(".search-wrapper").removeClass("col-md-4");
$(".search-wrapper").addClass("col-md-6");
}


function giftCertificatePage()
{
try {

function catSearchEnter(ele, id)
{ 
    if(event.key === 'Enter') {
        catSearch(id);        
    }
return false;

}
function catSearch(id)
{
var search = $('#catsearch').val();
//alert(" id= " + id + " search = " + search );
if ( search.length > 3 ) {
//alert(" id= " + id + " search = " + search );
event.preventDefault();
 window.location = "https://www.discoverbooks.com/sub-s/"+id+".htm?searching=Y&search="+search;

}
else
{
  alert("Search must be at least 4 letters long.  Please try again.");
}

}


//Cookie functions for having the popup only display after a given interval

function setCookie(c_name, value, exdays) {
  var exdate = new Date();
  exdate.setDate(exdate.getDate() + exdays);
  var c_value = escape(value) + ((exdays == null) ? "" : "; expires=" + exdate.toUTCString() + "; path=/");
  document.cookie = c_name + "=" + c_value;
}

function getCookie(c_name) {
    var i, x, y, ARRcookies = document.cookie.split(";");
    for (i = 0; i < ARRcookies.length; i++) {
        x = ARRcookies[i].substr(0, ARRcookies[i].indexOf("="));
        y = ARRcookies[i].substr(ARRcookies[i].indexOf("=") + 1);
        x = x.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, "");
        if (x == c_name) {
            return unescape(y);
        }
    }
    return null;
}


function newsletterSignup() 
{
try {
var path = location.pathname;
path  = path.toLowerCase()
 if ( path.indexOf("one-page-checkout.asp") != -1 	)
	{
//alert("checkout");
return;
}

     var cookie = getCookie("db_email_signup");
//alert(cookie );
     if (cookie == null )
{
$('#myModal').modal();
}
else
{
//alert("cookie set already");
}
     setCookie("db_email_signup", "displayed", 31);

     }
	catch(e)
    {
         //do nothing
    }
}

function checkLogoOnMobile()
{
try {
if ($('.logo__img.img-responsive').length  == 0 )
{
$('a.vol-logo__link').after( '  <a href="/"><img class="logo__img img-responsive" style="  margin-top: -100px !important;" src="https://www.discoverbooks.com/v/vspfiles/templates/255/images/template/COMBO_LOGO3.png?time=2019_09_30" ></a>' );
}

setTimeout(  checkLogoOnMobile, 100 );
     }
	catch(e)
    {
		var my_err = JSON.stringify(e, Object.getOwnPropertyNames(e));
		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
    }      
}

		my_err = my_err.replace(/\s/g, "_");
		$("body").after("<div id='err'></div>");
		$("#err").load("/v/vspfiles/schema/Generic/log_error_message.asp?error="+my_err );
    }

                                $('html').addClass('slideshow-active');/* Detects if the slideshow is on */
                                $("#slideshow-container").html($("#vSlider")); /* Replaces specific  section with the slideshow code */
                                var dataWidth = $('#slideshow-container').attr('data-max-width');
                                if (dataWidth == 'content') {
                                    $('html').addClass('slideshow-width-content');
                                } else if (dataWidth == 'mid') {
                                    $('html').addClass('slideshow-width-mid');
                                } else if (dataWidth == 'full') {
                                    $('html').addClass('slideshow-width-full');
                                } else if (dataWidth == 'max') {
                                    $('html').addClass('slideshow-width-max');
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    </script>
<!-- #############################################
                    Supported data-max-width:
                    ** "content" // Matches the default content width
                    ** "mid" // 1500px
                    ** "full" // 2000px
                    ** "max" // Full screen
                    "max" setting is not recommended as large 4k monitors will continue stretching the image full width and using larger images to compensate will cause long load times and higher bandwidth usage. "full" setting will be full screen for anyone with a 1080p monitor.
                    ############################################# -->

<tr>
<td><h1>Let the Stories Live On</h1><br/>
<inline style="font-size: 16px;">Discover Books was born out of the idea of diverting well-maintained used books from landfills and putting them in circulation for libraries and individuals to help fight the problem of global illiteracy.<br/><br/>Discover Books is dedicated to the sale of gently used books to support our goal of fighting global illiteracy and to Let the Stories Live On. We fundamentally believe that books exist to be read. However, many used books collect dust on shelves or live in darkened boxes. Even worse, it’s estimated that more than 2 billion pounds of books become waste each year. We help used books achieve their greatest purpose by collecting and reselling them to other readers through our online book store, donating books to those in need or recycling used books to become another useful good with a new story to tell. <br/><br/>We offer a wide selection of competitively priced books from top genres, including fiction, self-help, children’s books, romance, business, history, horror, textbooks, and more! All of our used books ship for free to the contiguous 48 U.S. states from our warehouse. Whether you’re looking to fight global illiteracy or you’re just looking for cheap or discount books online, you’ll find it with Discover Books. Enjoy browsing our inventory and our cheaper-than-Amazon prices, and thank you for letting the stories live on!</inline>
</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>
<inline style="font-size: 16px;"><br/>All our books ship for free to the contiguous 48 US states. Enjoy browsing our inventory and thank you for letting the stories live on!<br/></inline>

     if (document.getElementsByName("MailingList")[0]) {
        var emailSubscribeForm = document.getElementsByName("MailingList")[0];
        disableSubscribeSubmit = function() {
          emailSubscribeButton.removeAttribute("type");
        }
        forwardToMailingList = function(e) {
            var subscribeEmail = emailSubscribeForm.querySelector("input").value;
          sessionStorage.setItem("subEmailAddress", subscribeEmail);
          emailSubscribeForm.submit();
        }

        if (emailSubscribeForm.querySelector("button")) {
            var emailSubscribeButton = emailSubscribeForm.querySelector("button");
            emailSubscribeButton.addEventListener("click", forwardToMailingList);
